The pages (`D08-pages`) call `r, err := st.OpenProspectList(ctx, id, c.UserID, c.Email)` for every `GET` and `HEAD` of `/prospect-lists/<id>`, `c` the request's caller, showing `r.ByBasis`, `r.Changes` and each member's `m.Basis`, `store.ErrNotFound` answering the not-found page, and the landing `st.ListProspectLists(ctx, name, id, paging.DefaultLimit+1)` and `st.ListPasses(ctx, started, id, paging.DefaultLimit+1)`, each position decoded from its query parameter or empty; for a before-token `ks, err := st.ListProspectListsReversed(ctx, name, id, paging.DefaultLimit+1)` and `ps, err := st.ListPassesReversed(ctx, started, id, paging.DefaultLimit+1)`, for `last` the same with `"", ""`, each window the first `paging.DefaultLimit` reversed and underflowing when no more came back; the candidate page `st.GetCandidate`, `st.Links`, `st.Estimates`, `st.GetJudgments` for the judgments card, `st.Rulings`, `st.Rejects`, `st.Enrichments`, whose results' website decision (R-QJ7S-Y7FR) its website fields show and whose `store.BasisOf` and `store.KindsOf` its enrichments card shows, and `f, err := st.GetFacts(ctx, id)`, a record whose `ProspectListName` is empty naming a deleted prospect list, `store.ErrNotFound` from `GetFacts` the facts card's empty state (`D08-pages`), and `store.ClassifiedBy(f.Categories)`, `store.RecordsDiffer(f.Categories, f.Records)` and `f.Records` what the facts card shows of the classification, the prospect list page likewise showing `r.Classified` and each member's `store.ClassifiedBy(m.Facts.Categories)` and `m.Facts.WebsiteSource`, and `ps, err := st.Pages(ctx, id)`; the text page `/candidates/<id>/pages/<n>` calls `pg, err := st.GetPage(ctx, id, n)`, `store.ErrNotFound` answering the not-found page. The tools also call `cs, err := st.ListCandidates(ctx, store.CandidateFilter{Name: "trucking"}, created, id, n+1)`, the `n`th candidate's `c.Created.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339)` and `c.ID` giving `next`, and `ps, err := st.ListPasses(ctx, started, id, n+1)`, the `n`th pass's `p.Started.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339)` and `p.ID` giving `next`; `decide` calls `r, cs, err := st.Decide(ctx, store.Ruling{Kind: store.KindMerge, Candidate: a, Other: b, Why: why, UserID: c.UserID, UserEmail: c.Email})` and `exclude` the same with `store.KindExclude` and no `Other`. Any error matching `store.ErrUnreachable` is answered with `store.Unreachable`.

The campaign delivery (`D06-passes`), `v` the delivered event's payload, records `err := st.AddCampaignUse(ctx, v.Candidate, v.Campaign, v.ProspectList, d.Event.Time)` for `campaign.member.added`, `st.RejectCampaignUse(ctx, v.Candidate, v.Campaign, v.Reason, d.Event.Time)` for `campaign.member.rejected` and `st.DeliverCampaignUse(ctx, v.Candidate, v.Campaign, d.Event.Time)` for `campaign.piece.delivered`, a delivery whose candidate or campaign is empty answering `Skip` before any call, a nil error `OK`, `store.ErrNotFound` `Skip`, and any other error `Fail`. The `candidate` tool and the candidate page (`D10-tools`, `D08-pages`) call `us, err := st.CampaignUses(ctx, id)` beside `st.Pages`, one entry or row per use in the order answered, each `u.Campaign`, `u.ProspectList`, `u.Added`, `u.Rejected`, `u.Reason` and `u.Delivered`, a zero time shown as none and an empty `us` as no campaign. A prospect list read applies `MinCampaignAgeDays` to its members by their uses' effective times (`D14-prospect-lists`).

The facts from the candidate. Current: `c, err := st.GetCandidate(ctx, id)` answers the display up to `c.Email`, `c.People` as `[]sources.Person` and `c.Names`; `f, err := st.GetFacts(ctx, id)` answers `f.KnownSince`, `f.NewestFiling` and `f.WebsiteSource`, `check`, `search` or empty. Proposed: `c.NameSource`, `c.AddressSource`, `c.PhoneSource` and `c.EmailSource`, each a source identifier, with `c.NameRecord` and the other three record keys; each `x` of `c.People` read as `x.Value.Name`, `x.Value.Role`, `x.Source` and `x.Key`; `c.OtherNames`, `c.OtherAddresses`, `c.OtherPhones` and `c.OtherEmails`, each `o` read as `o.Value`, `o.Source` and `o.Key`, which the card (`D08-pages`) and the `candidate` tool (`D10-tools`) show beside each fact; `f.KnownSinceSource`, `f.NewestFilingSource` and `f.WebsiteCheckedURL`, and `f.WebsiteSource` also `store.WebsiteByPlaces`. A test of the decision records, on a candidate a walk's `RecordSweep` founded from a place with a website, results of `sources.WebsiteCheck` whose `Inputs` are `website.Inputs(site, email)` and reads `GetFacts`; a test of `0026` reads `0026_fact_sources.sql` as the others, and one of the previous release's rows across it writes `candidates` and `facts` rows with `d.Write` naming only the first twenty-five files' columns.

The page text repair (`D06-passes`, page text outcome 6). Current: `st.AddPage(ctx, store.Page{Candidate: id, URL: u, Title: t, Text: x, Size: n, UserID: c.UserID, UserEmail: c.Email})` records a copy. Proposed: the copy also carries `Reason: g.Reason`, empty or a `website` reason code, with `Text` empty beside a code; the repair walks `ps, err := st.PendingRefetches(ctx, after, passes.BatchSize)`, `after` the last `p.Number` it was given, starting at 0 and ending at an empty page, fetches each `p.URL`, adds the newer copy with `st.AddPage(ctx, store.Page{Candidate: p.Candidate, URL: p.URL, Title: g.Title, Text: g.Text, Reason: g.Reason, Size: g.Size, UserID: p.UserID, UserEmail: p.UserEmail})`, and settles it with `st.SettleRefetch(ctx, p.Number, store.RefetchRead)`, `store.RefetchUnreadable` or `store.RefetchFailed`. A test of `0027` reads `0027_page_reasons.sql` as the others; one of the previous release's rows across it opens a database with the first twenty-six files, writes pages with `d.Write` naming `0013`'s columns, opens it with every file and walks `PendingRefetches`.

A test opens its own database with `db.Open` and the root package's `Migrations()`, builds `store.New(d, store.Config{Now: clock, Rand: rnd})`, replaces the ZIP table with `candidates.ZipDrive()`, attaches observations it builds from `sources.Reading` values, judges with `st.Judge(ctx, ids)` and, page by page, `st.JudgeAll(ctx, after, n, at)` and reads `st.GetJudgments`, records, finishes and fails passes, creates, edits, reads, rejects in and deletes prospect lists, moves the clock to move a prospect list's filing-age date, pages each list window by window, flips `d.SetFailing(true)`, and reopens the path to prove what persists. A test of a busy write opens a second handle `d2` with `db.Open` on the same path, holds `d2.Write(ctx, fn)` open, its `fn` waiting on a channel the test holds, builds the store with `store.Config{Now: clock, Rand: rnd, Sleep: sleep}`, `sleep` recording each duration and, on the call the case names, releasing `fn` or cancelling the call's context, and calls `st.Attach(ctx, run, false, batch)`, `st.MarkGone(ctx, scraper, run, "", 500)`, `st.Derive(ctx, enrichers.KindLinkFacts, "", false, 500)`, `st.JudgeAll(ctx, "", 500, at)`, `st.FillFacts(ctx, "", 500)` or `st.PrunePasses(ctx, 1, 500)`, comparing the recorded durations with `store.WriteBackoff` doubled and their number with `store.WriteRetries`. A test of a window's plan, `d` the handle its store was built over, runs `d.Read(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error { rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, "EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN "+store.ProspectListsAfter, "x", "y", 26); ... })` and reads each row's `id`, `parent`, `notused` and `detail`; a test of a statement's rows runs `tx.QueryContext(ctx, store.ProspectListsPreceding, name, id, n)` the same way and compares the first column of each row with the `ID`s `st.ListProspectListsReversed(ctx, name, id, n)` returns, and likewise for the other statements. A test of `0012` reads `fs.ReadFile(candidates.Migrations(), "0012_facts.sql")`; a test of the previous release's rows opens a database with a file system holding only the first eleven files, writes rows with `d.Write` as that release would, opens it again with `candidates.Migrations()` and asks `st.GetJudgments`, `st.GetPass`, `st.Enrichments` and `st.GetFacts`, then calls `st.FillFacts` and asks `st.GetFacts` again; a test of `0013` reads `0013_site_text.sql` the same way, and one of the previous release's rows across it opens a database with the first twelve files, writes candidates, enrichments, facts rows and passes with `d.Write` as release-44 writes them, before the migration and again after it, and asks `st.GetPass`, `st.GetFacts`, `st.Enrichments` and `st.Pages`. A test of `0014` reads `0014_brief.sql` the same way; one of the previous release's rows across it opens a database with the first thirteen files, writes candidates, enrichments and facts rows with `d.Write` as release-46 writes them, opens it again with `candidates.Migrations()` and asks `st.GetFacts` and `st.Enrichments`; and one of a mixed release inserts such a facts row after the migration and asks `st.GetFacts`. A test of `0015` reads `0015_website_search.sql` the same way; one of the previous release's rows across it opens a database with the first fourteen files, writes candidates, enrichments and facts rows with `d.Write` as release-50 writes them, rows with a site and without one among them, opens it again with `candidates.Migrations()` and asks `st.GetFacts` and `st.Enrichments`; and one of a mixed release inserts such facts rows after the migration and upserts one, `website_search_at` among its columns, over a row this release wrote with a source, and asks `st.GetFacts`. A test of the pages adds copies with `st.AddPage`, twenty distinct URLs, a twenty-first and a repeat, races adds from goroutines at nineteen, merges and splits with `st.Decide`, and asks `st.Pages`, `st.GetPage` and `st.CountPages`; a test of the targeted walks calls `st.DueAmong` and `st.DeriveAmong` with ids holding an unknown, a merged and a repeated id, with and without `force`, and `st.Subject` of a candidate with and without a facts row and with and without pages, a URL kept twice among them, and with and without a website check sharing a URL with its current inputs, a newer one of other inputs among its results. A test of the enrichments records results with `st.AddEnrichments`, building each `Found` with `json.Marshal` of an `enrichers.LinkFound`, `enrichers.MinutesFound`, `website.Result` `enrichers.BriefFound` (a good brief, a failed one recorded after a good one, only failed ones, a good one with no categories recorded after one with some, and categories repeating or overlapping the link facts') or `enrichers.WebsiteSearchFound` (a confirmed site, a confirmation whose state is `none`, an empty URL, a URL with no confirmation and a failed run, each recorded before and after a website check that found a site or none, after a confirmed one and after an unfound one), runs `st.Derive` with and without `force`, changes a candidate's links, ZIP or email between calls, takes `st.EnrichmentMark` before and after, merges and splits with `st.Decide`, ingests and judges, and asks `st.Enrichments`, `st.DueEnrichments`, `st.EnrichmentAfter`, `st.GetFacts`, `f.Records` among it, `st.GetJudgments` and `st.ReadProspectList`, `r.Classified` among it, what each left. A test of the campaign uses adds, rejects and delivers with `st.AddCampaignUse`, `st.RejectCampaignUse` and `st.DeliverCampaignUse` in every order and twice over, with times in one second and across seconds, an empty prospect list or reason, an empty candidate or campaign, a zero time, an unknown candidate, and a candidate merged once and twice with `st.Decide`, races writes of one candidate and campaign from goroutines, asks `st.CampaignUses` of the survivor and of the merged candidates, and closes the handle and opens the path again to ask `st.CampaignUses` once more; a test of `0016` reads `0016_campaign_uses.sql` as the others, and one of the previous release's rows across it opens a database with the first fifteen files, writes candidates with `d.Write` as that release writes them, opens it again with `candidates.Migrations()` and asks `st.CampaignUses`. A test of the classification calls `store.ClassifiedBy(cs)` and `store.RecordsDiffer(cs, rs)` directly with sets it builds and compares the answers. A test of serving holds `d.Write(ctx, fn)` open, its `fn` waiting on a channel the test holds, starts `st.Attach(ctx, run, false, batch)`, `st.Decide(ctx, r)` and `st.Judge(ctx, ids)` each in a goroutine of its own, and calls `st.GetCandidate`, `st.Links` and `st.ReadProspectList`, each called a fixed number of times, yielding between calls, and each answering before the test releases `fn`; were a read to wait for the write, the test would never release it and would fail at the package's timeout, so the test waits on no clock.

A test of `0017` reads `0017_prospect_lists.sql` the same way. One of the previous release's rows across it opens a database with the first sixteen files, writes with `d.Write`, as the previous release writes them, six rows of `cohorts` with filters, orders and sizes, the `rejects` that stand on them and some that are un-rejected, `campaign_uses` naming one of them, `assessments` for every judged candidate and passes whose kind, origin and trigger are `rebuild` or whose kind is `enrich_cohort` with a `coh_` target, reads every `ReadProspectList` the way that release's store would read the same rows (the members its definition gives, which the fixture is built to know), opens the database again with `candidates.Migrations()` and asks `st.ListProspectLists`, `st.ReadProspectList(ctx, store.MapListID(id))` of each, `st.Rejects`, `st.CampaignUses`, `st.GetJudgments` and `st.GetPass`, comparing each with what it wrote: the same members in the same order under `pl_` ids, the same standing rejects, the uses naming the mapped list, the judgments' fields equal to the old rows' judgment columns, and the passes answering `ingest` and `enrich_target` with `judged` equal to the old `rescored`. A second test, on a database every file has migrated, writes with `d.Write` rows shaped as the previous release writes them (`rejects` naming `cohort` and not `prospect_list`, `passes` naming `rescored` and neither `judged` nor `scraper`, `campaign_uses` naming `cohort`, and rows into `cohorts` and `assessments`), each write succeeding, and asks what the store then answers. A test of `store.MapListID` calls it with the cases R-ZLDX-NAVV lists.

A test of the opens opens and reads prospect lists with `st.OpenProspectList(ctx, id, "u", "e")` and `st.ReadProspectList(ctx, id)` while it moves the clock, changes their definitions, candidates, results, rejects and rulings between opens, opens with an empty user id, an unknown id and a deleted list's id, races two opens of one list from goroutines, asks `st.Opens(ctx, id)` of each list, a deleted one's among them, before and after every other kind of write, and opens the path again to ask `st.ReadProspectList` once more. A test of `0019` reads `0019_opens.sql` as the others; one of the previous release's rows across it opens a database with the first eighteen files, writes candidates, judgments and prospect lists with `d.Write` as that release writes them, opens it again with `candidates.Migrations()` and asks `st.GetProspectList`, `st.ReadProspectList` and `st.Opens`; and one of a mixed release, on a database every file has migrated and some opens recorded, inserts, edits and deletes `prospect_lists` rows with `d.Write` as the previous release does and asks the same, and `st.OpenProspectList` of the deleted one.

`Run` builds the store with `store.New(d, store.Config{Now: p.Now, Rand: p.Rand, Origin: s.RouteOrigin, Sleep: sleep})`, `sleep` being `p.Sleep`, or a pause of real time that ends early when its context is done when `p.Sleep` is nil (`D01-layout-and-run-seam`). A targeted `drive_route` pass (`D06-passes`) asks `ds, err := st.DueAmong(ctx, enrichers.KindDriveRoute, ids, force)`, builds each `s, err := st.Subject(ctx, d.Candidate)`, whose `s.Origin` is the store's, and records each route with `st.AddEnrichments(ctx, []store.Enrichment{{Candidate: d.Candidate, Kind: enrichers.KindDriveRoute, Inputs: d.Inputs, Source: r.Source, Found: r.Found, Cost: r.Cost}})`. The `candidate` tool, the candidate page and the prospect list reads answer `f.Minutes` with `f.MinutesSource`, `store.MinutesByRoute` or `store.MinutesByZip`, and, when the route decided, `f.Miles` and `f.RouteAddress` (`D10-tools`, `D08-pages`). A test of the drive decision builds a store with an `Origin` of its own, records `drive_minutes` results with `st.DeriveAmong` and `drive_route` results with `st.AddEnrichments`, each `Found` `json.Marshal` of an `enrichers.RouteFound` (routed, unroutable with a why, failed with an error, and one found from other inputs), attaches a changed street with `st.Attach`, asks `st.GetFacts`, then opens the path again under a store with another `Origin`, calls `st.FillFacts` and asks again. A test of `0018` reads `0018_drive_route.sql` as the others; one of the previous release's rows across it opens a database with the first seventeen files, writes candidates and facts rows with `d.Write` as that release writes them, minutes and none among them, opens it again with `candidates.Migrations()` and asks `st.GetFacts`; and one of a mixed release inserts such facts rows after the migration, `drive_route_at` among the columns for a routed candidate, upserts one over a row this release wrote with a route, and asks `st.GetFacts`.
The headcounts (revenue bands decisions 4 to 8 and 19). Current: `f, err := st.GetFacts(ctx, id)` answers the link facts up to `f.PowerUnits` and `f.Drivers` and the brief's `f.Brief`, and `j, err := st.GetJudgments(ctx, id)` answers the revenue estimate up to `j.RevenueConfidence`; `st.Estimates(ctx, id)` answers one estimate per source that gave one. Proposed: `f.Headcounts`, each `h` a `sources.Headcount` read as `h.Scraper`, `h.Kind`, `h.Count` and `h.AsOf`, and `f.BriefLabels` are answered beside them, and `j.HeadcountLow`, `j.HeadcountHigh` and `j.HeadcountBasis` beside `j.RevenueConfidence`; the judge (`D13-judging`) reads `judging.HeadcountEstimate(f.Headcounts, f.BriefLabels, f.Categories)` and `judging.PickHeadcount(f.Headcounts)` from the facts row it already reads, and `st.Estimates(ctx, id)` answers its `headcount` estimate among the sources', in source order; the `candidate` tool, the candidate page and the prospect list reads answer the new fields from `f` and `j` and `m.Facts` and `m.Judgments` (`D10-tools`, `D08-pages`, `D14-prospect-lists`). A test of the projection records `link_facts` results with `st.AddEnrichments`, each `Found` `json.Marshal` of an `enrichers.LinkFound` whose `Headcounts` hold `enrichers.Headcount{Scraper: ppp.Name, Kind: sources.HeadcountJobs, Count: 26, Date: "2020-04-28"}` and one with no date, and brief results whose `enrichers.BriefFound` has `Labels`, good and failed, and asks `st.GetFacts`. A test of `0020` reads `0020_headcounts.sql` as the others; one of the previous release's rows across it opens a database with the first nineteen files, writes candidates, facts rows and judgments with `d.Write` as that release writes them, opens it again with `candidates.Migrations()` and asks `st.GetFacts` and `st.GetJudgments`, then records results, calls `st.FillFacts` and asks again; and one of a mixed release, after `st.Judge` has written a headcount and a write has given a row headcounts and labels, upserts facts rows and judgments naming only the earlier columns and asks `st.GetFacts` and `st.GetJudgments`.

The link keys (form5500 decisions 14, 15 and 23). Current: a link's `l.Reading` carries `l.Reading.NameKey`, and a row finds a candidate by it with its state and ZIP. Proposed: `ls, err := st.Links(ctx, id)` answers each `l` with `l.Reading.EINKey` and `l.Reading.NameKeys` as `st.Attach` recorded them, and `l.Reason` `store.LinkEIN` for a link a row attached by its EIN key (`D12-ingest`), which the `candidate` tool and the candidate page show (`D10-tools`, `D08-pages`). A test of `0021` reads `0021_link_keys.sql` as the others; one of the previous release's rows across it opens a database with the first twenty files, writes candidates and links with `d.Write` as that release writes them, opens it again with `candidates.Migrations()` and asks `st.Links`; and one of a mixed release, after `st.Attach` has recorded links with EIN keys and name keys, inserts a link and updates one with `d.Write` naming only `0002`'s columns and asks `st.Links`, on that store and after opening the path again.

The OSHA ITA figures (OSHA ITA outcomes 11, 12 and 16, OSHA ITA P6). Current: `f, err := st.GetFacts(ctx, id)` answers `f.Headcounts`, each `h` read as `h.Scraper`, `h.Kind`, `h.Count` and `h.AsOf`, and `ls, err := st.Links(ctx, id)` answers each `l` with `l.Reading` up to `l.Reading.GovernmentMatch`. Proposed: each `h` is also read as `h.Hours`, nil when its row gave no hours, one element per link, the sites of one year never summed, and each `l.Reading` also as `l.Reading.Establishment`, `l.Reading.Year`, `l.Reading.Employees`, `l.Reading.Hours` and `l.Reading.Headcount.Hours`, as `st.Attach` recorded them (`D12-ingest`); the judge sums a year's sites from `f.Headcounts` (`D13-judging`), and the candidate page and the `candidate` tool show the figures and hours from `ls` and `f` (`D08-pages`, `D10-tools`). A test of the hours records `link_facts` results with `st.AddEnrichments`, each `Found` `json.Marshal` of an `enrichers.LinkFound` whose `Headcounts` hold `enrichers.Headcount{Scraper: oshaita.Name, Kind: sources.HeadcountEmployees, Count: 23, Date: "2025-12-31", Hours: &hours}`, an identical pair and one with nil `Hours`, and asks `st.GetFacts`, on that store and after opening the path again; and one of the previous release's rows sets a facts row's `headcounts` with `d.Write` to elements with no `hours` member, as that release writes them, calls `st.FillFacts` and asks `st.GetFacts`.

The headcount series and trend (headcount trend decisions 1 to 10). Current: `j, err := st.GetJudgments(ctx, id)` answers the headcount up to `j.HeadcountBasis`, and a list's `store.ProspectListFilters` ends its website pair at `WebsiteUnknown`. Proposed: `j.HeadcountSeries` answers each `sr` as `sr.Kind`, `sr.Scraper` and `sr.Figures`, each `fg` read as `fg.Year` and `fg.Count`, and `j.Trend` one of `judging.TrendGrowing`, `judging.TrendFlat`, `judging.TrendShrinking` and `judging.TrendUnknown`, with `j.TrendKind`, `*j.TrendFromYear`, `*j.TrendToYear`, `*j.TrendFrom`, `*j.TrendTo` and `*j.TrendChange` when it is known; a member `m` of `r, err := st.ReadProspectList(ctx, id)` carries the same in `m.Judgments`; `st.CreateProspectList(ctx, store.ProspectList{..., Filters: store.ProspectListFilters{Trend: []string{judging.TrendGrowing}, TrendUnknown: false}, Order: []store.ProspectListOrder{{Field: store.OrderTrend, Direction: store.DirectionDesc}}})` keeps a list of the growing businesses, largest growth first; and a test reads what `st.Judge(ctx, ids)` wrote through `st.GetJudgments` on a store over a second handle for the same path, and writes the previous release's `judgments` upserts and `prospect_lists` rows with `d.Write` naming only the columns and members that release knows.

## REQUIREMENTS

- R-47BB-A47P: The `internal/store` package, imported from the path `github.com/ikigenba/ikigenba/candidates/internal/store` with the package name `store`, MUST export `type Config struct { Now func() time.Time; Rand io.Reader; Origin string; Sleep func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) }`, `type Store`, and `func New(d *db.DB, cfg Config) *Store`, where `db` is appkit's package `github.com/ikigenba/ikigenba/appkit/db` and `context`, `io` and `time` are the standard library's packages; `Origin` being the address the drive of the `drive_route` kind is measured from, which the store gives the subject of every candidate it holds, and `Sleep` the pause the store waits with before it writes a busy write unit again, nil meaning it never writes one again.
- R-QEC7-F4GZ: The `internal/store` package MUST export `type Candidate struct { ID, Status, MergedInto, Name string; Names []string; Street, City, State, Zip, County, Phone, Email string; People []sources.Sourced[sources.Person]; Class string; Cargo []string; Created, Updated time.Time; NameSource, NameRecord, AddressSource, AddressRecord, PhoneSource, PhoneRecord, EmailSource, EmailRecord string; OtherNames []sources.Sourced[string]; OtherAddresses []sources.Sourced[sources.Address]; OtherPhones, OtherEmails []sources.Sourced[string] }`, with exactly these fields in this order, where `sources` is candidates' `internal/sources` (`D01-layout-and-run-seam`, `D05-sources`); a `Candidate` is one candidate: its id, its status, the id it was merged into (empty unless merged), its display name, every name its links use with the display name first, its display street, city, state, ZIP, county, phone and email, the people its display names, each with the source identifier and the key of the record it was taken from, its class tag, its cargo tags, when it was created and when it was last changed; the source identifier and the record key its display name, its display address (the street, city, state and ZIP together), its display phone and its display email were taken from, each empty when its value was taken from none; and every other value its records hold for the display name, the address, the phone and the email, each with the source identifier and the key of the record that holds it.
- R-F1DI-OTWO: The `internal/store` package MUST export `type Link struct { Candidate, Scraper, SourceID, Run, Digest string; Reading sources.Reading; Reason string; Confidence float64; FirstSeen, LastSeen time.Time; Gone bool; Walk, Park string }`, with exactly these fields in this order; a `Link` is one row's attachment: the id of the candidate it is attached to, the row's scraper and source id, the id of the run it was last seen in, its digest as last seen, the source's reading of it as last seen, how it attached and with what confidence, when it was first and last seen, whether it was absent from the latest run of its scraper that a pass read, and, for a link whose `Scraper` is `places.Name` (`D19-walk`), the pass id of the newest walk that found its place and the id of the park that walk found it in, both empty for every other link; the `Run` and `Digest` of a link a walk made being what `D19-walk` states.
- R-OTPV-DHNQ: The `internal/store` package MUST export `type Judgments struct { Candidate string; Operating *float64; OperatingKey int64; Evidence []sources.Evidence; RevenueLow, RevenueHigh *int64; RevenueConfidence *float64; HeadcountLow, HeadcountHigh *int64; HeadcountBasis string; HeadcountSeries []judging.Series; Trend, TrendKind string; TrendFromYear, TrendToYear, TrendFrom, TrendTo, TrendChange *int64; Excluded bool; Rules int64; Calculated time.Time }`, with exactly these fields in this order; a `Judgments` is one candidate's two judgments as the last judge wrote them (`D13-judging`), and nothing the enrichers found: the candidate's id; its operating confidence, from 0 to 1 (nil when it has no operating evidence); its operating key, the integer form of its operating confidence; its operating evidence; its combined revenue low, revenue high (nil when no estimate gives one) and revenue confidence (all three nil when it has no estimate); the low and the high of the headcount the judge picked from its facts row's headcounts, equal when they are one count, and that headcount's basis, the kinds and scrapers that gave it (nil, nil and empty when it has no headcount); its headcount series, one `judging.Series` for each kind with a figure, each holding its kind, the scraper behind its newest figure and its figures, one per year, oldest year first (`D13-judging`), a non-nil slice of length zero when it has none; its trend, one of `judging.TrendGrowing`, `judging.TrendFlat`, `judging.TrendShrinking` and `judging.TrendUnknown`, never empty; and the trend's basis, the kind it was read in, the comparison year and the newest year, the figures of those two years and the change from the first figure to the second (empty and five nils when the trend is `judging.TrendUnknown`); whether the owner's standing ruling excludes it; the rules version; and when it was calculated.
- R-4CRF-R3RJ: The `internal/store` package MUST export `type Ruling struct { Kind, Candidate, Other string; Keys []string; Why, UserID, UserEmail string; At time.Time }`, with exactly these fields in this order; a `Ruling` is one of the owner's rulings: its kind, the id of the candidate it names, the id of the other candidate (empty for `exclude` and `include`), the source keys a `split` moved (empty for other kinds), the sentence given as why, the user id and email of who made it, and when.
- R-VJ58-3H5L: The `internal/store` package MUST export `type Pass struct { ID, Kind, Origin, Trigger, Target, EnrichKind, Scraper, ParcelRun, Status, Reason string; Started, Finished time.Time; Rows, New, Changed, Unchanged, Gone, Created, Linked, Judged, Unranged, Derived, Due, Fetched, HTTPS, HTTP, None, Runs, RunsFailed, Pages, Cost, ParcelsRead, ByLabel, ByValue, ByCandidate, ParksCreated, ParksUpdated, ParksGone, ParksGrown, ParksWalked, Circles, Requests, Found, NewCandidates, SearchDifferent, SearchStands, SearchNone int64; CeilingReached bool; UserID, UserEmail, RequestID, Delivery string }`, with exactly these fields in this order; a `Pass` is one pass: its id, its kind (what it does), its origin (what started it), its trigger (the run id, the cron trigger's slug, `TriggerIngest`, `TriggerJudgeTool`, `TriggerEnrich`, `TriggerParkRules`, `TriggerWalk`, or the `ID` of the pass that started it), its target (the id of the prospect list or the candidate a pass whose `Kind` is `KindEnrichTarget` walks, empty for every other pass), its enrich kind (the kind such a pass walks, empty for every other pass), its scraper (the name of the one scraper a pass whose `Origin` is `OriginIngest` ingests, empty when it ingests every scraper and for every other pass), its parcel run (the `mn_parcels` run a pass whose `Kind` is `KindParks` derived the parks from, set when that pass ends `StatusDone`, empty while it runs, once it failed and for every other pass), its status, its failure reason (empty unless failed), when it was recorded and when it ended (the zero `time.Time` while running), its thirty-five counts (each 0 unless done, but a failed walk's five walk counts and `Cost`, which hold what it spent), `Cost` among them a sum in nano-dollars, `ParksWalked`, `Circles`, `Requests`, `Found` and `NewCandidates` a walk's parks walked, circles answered, Places requests made, places found and new candidates (`D19-walk`), and the last three the number of `website_search` results it recorded whose case is, in turn, a different site, the site on record and no site (`D06-passes`, `D16-enrichers`), whether a walk stopped at the walk ceiling (false unless its `Kind` is `KindWalk` and it is done), its starter's user id and email, the request id it was started under, and the id of the bus event whose delivery started it (empty for a pass no delivery started).

- R-ONLR-C520: The `internal/store` package MUST export `type Park struct { ID, Name, County, Town, Zip, State string; Number int64; Lat, Lon, Radius float64; Parcels, BuildingValue int64; Minutes *int64; GrownFrom int64; Walked time.Time; WalkRun string; Found, NewCandidates int64; CeilingReached bool; Run string; Created, Updated time.Time }`, with exactly these fields in this order; a `Park` is one park (`D18-parks`): its id, its name at the read, its county, its town, its ZIP (empty when none of its parcels has one), its state (`parks.StateActive` or `parks.StateGone`), its number within its county and town, its center's latitude and longitude in degrees, its radius in meters, its parcel count, the sum of its parcels' known building values in dollars, its drive minutes at the read (nil when unknown), the parcel count it had before it grew (0 unless it grew), when its newest walk walked it, that walk's pass id, the number of places that walk found in it, how many of them founded a candidate, and whether the walk ceiling cut that walk's sweep of it (the zero `time.Time`, empty, 0, 0 and false while it is unwalked; `D19-walk`), the `mn_parcels` run of the derivation that last created or updated it, when it was minted, and when an `ApplyParks` last created, updated or marked it gone.

- R-5Q9X-61SP: The `internal/store` package MUST export `type ParkFilter struct { County, Walked, Gone string }`, with exactly these fields in this order; a `ParkFilter` is one view of the parks: a county, empty for every county, and a walked word and a gone word, each empty for its default; which parks pass it is `D18-parks`' (R-TMFI-X33R).

- R-56RJ-1PXL: Every id a method of `*Store` mints, for a pass, a candidate or a park, MUST be made by reading exactly 8 bytes from `Config.Rand` for each attempt, at most 8 attempts, writing the kind's prefix (`PassIDPrefix`, `CandidateIDPrefix` or `ParkIDPrefix`) and those bytes as 16 lowercase hexadecimal digits in the order read, and drawing again when the attempt's id is the id of a record of the same kind the store holds or, for a park, the id the same call minted for an earlier park; so that a `Rand` yielding `0x5a` for every byte gives a store holding no pass the pass id `pass_5a5a5a5a5a5a5a5a`, and a `Rand` yielding `0x5a` eight times and then `0x6b` eight times gives a store already holding `pass_5a5a5a5a5a5a5a5a` a pass with the id `pass_6b6b6b6b6b6b6b6b`, and an `ApplyParks` of a plan holding two `New` elements, on a store holding no park whose `Rand` yields `0x5a` for every byte, mints `park_5a5a5a5a5a5a5a5a` for the first and finds all 8 attempts for the second taken; and when all 8 attempts give ids the store holds, having read exactly 64 bytes, or a read from `Config.Rand` fails, the call minting MUST return the zero values of its results and an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrUnreachable)` is true, and change no record.
- R-MAA1-OFX6: Every id `CreateProspectList` mints MUST be made by reading exactly 8 bytes from `Config.Rand` for each attempt, at most 8 attempts, writing `ProspectListIDPrefix` and those bytes as 16 lowercase hexadecimal digits in the order read, and drawing again when the attempt's id is the `ID` of a prospect list the store holds or the `ProspectList` of a reject record the store holds; so that a `Rand` yielding `0x5a` for every byte gives a store holding no prospect list and no reject record the prospect list id `pl_5a5a5a5a5a5a5a5a`, and a `Rand` yielding `0x5a` eight times and then `0x6b` eight times gives a store whose only record naming `pl_5a5a5a5a5a5a5a5a` is a reject record of that prospect list, deleted, a prospect list with the id `pl_6b6b6b6b6b6b6b6b`; and when all 8 attempts give such ids, having read exactly 64 bytes, or a read from `Config.Rand` fails, `CreateProspectList` MUST return the zero `ProspectList` and an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrUnreachable)` is true, and change no record.
- R-OHI9-FACJ: candidates' design defines a **valid new pass** as a `Pass` `p` whose `Trigger` is not empty and which, whatever its other fields, either has an empty `Target`, an empty `EnrichKind` and one of these: `Kind` `KindIngest`, `Origin` `OriginIngest`, `Trigger` equal to `TriggerIngest`, an empty `Delivery` and a non-empty `UserID`, whatever its `Scraper`; or an empty `Scraper` and one of these: `Kind` `KindIngest`, `Origin` `OriginRun` and a non-empty `Delivery`; `Kind` `KindJudge`, `Origin` `OriginCron`, a `Trigger` other than `TriggerIngest` and `TriggerJudgeTool` and a non-empty `Delivery`; `Kind` `KindJudge`, `Origin` `OriginJudge`, `Trigger` equal to `TriggerJudgeTool`, an empty `Delivery` and a non-empty `UserID`; `Kind` `KindEnrich`, `Origin` `OriginEnrich`, `Trigger` equal to `TriggerEnrich`, an empty `Delivery` and a non-empty `UserID`; `Kind` `KindEnrich`, `Origin` `OriginPass`, a `Trigger` that `ValidPassID` accepts and an empty `Delivery`; `Kind` `KindJudge`, `Origin` `OriginPass`, a `Trigger` that `ValidPassID` accepts and an empty `Delivery`; `Kind` `KindWalk`, `Origin` `OriginWalk`, `Trigger` equal to `TriggerWalk`, an empty `Delivery` and a non-empty `UserID`; `Kind` `KindParks`, `Origin` `OriginRun` and a non-empty `Delivery`; `Kind` `KindParks`, `Origin` `OriginRules`, `Trigger` equal to `TriggerParkRules`, an empty `Delivery` and a non-empty `UserID`; or `Kind` `KindParks`, `Origin` `OriginStart` and an empty `Delivery`; or has an empty `Scraper`, `Kind` `KindEnrichTarget`, `Origin` `OriginEnrich`, `Trigger` equal to `TriggerEnrich`, a `Target` that `ValidProspectListID` or `ValidCandidateID` accepts, an `EnrichKind` that is an element of `enrichers.Kinds()` (`D16-enrichers`), an empty `Delivery` and a non-empty `UserID`; a pass's **scope** as its `Scraper` when its `Kind` is `KindIngest` and its `Origin` is `OriginIngest`, its `Trigger` when its `Kind` is `KindIngest` and its `Origin` is any other, its `Target` when its `Kind` is `KindEnrichTarget`, and the empty string, the corpus, for every other `Kind`; a pass **blocks** `p` when its `Status` is `StatusRunning`, its `Kind` equals `p.Kind`, its scope equals `p`'s scope and, when that `Kind` is `KindIngest`, its `Origin` is `OriginIngest` exactly when `p.Origin` is, so that an ingest a delivery recorded for a run whose id equals a scraper's name never blocks, nor is blocked by, an ingest of that scraper the tool started, a running pass whose `Kind` is `KindParks` blocks every valid new pass whose `Kind` is `KindParks`, whatever the two passes' origins, and no pass of another kind, and a running pass whose `Kind` is `KindWalk` blocks every valid new pass whose `Kind` is `KindWalk` and no pass of another kind; and a pass's **pass order** as descending order of `Started` and, among passes whose `Started` is equal, descending byte order of `ID`; every requirement of candidates' design that names a valid new pass, a pass's scope, a pass blocking one, or pass order MUST denote these.
- R-VP8Q-0BV2: When `p` is a valid new pass, the store holds no pass that blocks `p` (R-OHI9-FACJ), `p.Delivery` is empty or no pass the store holds has `Delivery` equal to it, and `p.Origin` is not `OriginRun` or no pass the store holds whose `Origin` is `OriginRun` has `Trigger` equal to `p.Trigger`, `AddPass(ctx, p)` MUST return a nil error and a `Pass` whose `ID` satisfies `ValidPassID` and differs from every pass's the store holds, whose `Status` is `StatusRunning`, whose `Kind`, `Origin`, `Trigger`, `Target`, `EnrichKind`, `Scraper`, `UserID`, `UserEmail`, `RequestID` and `Delivery` equal `p`'s, whose `Started` equals `Config.Now()` read during the call in UTC truncated to the second, whose `Finished` is the zero `time.Time`, whose `Reason` and `ParcelRun` are empty and whose thirty-five counts are 0, whose `CeilingReached` is false, whatever `p`'s other fields are; and every later `GetPass(ctx, id)` with that `ID`, on that store and on a store over a later handle `db.Open` returns for the same path with the root package's `Migrations()`, MUST return that `Pass` until a later call changes it; so that a valid new pass is recorded while passes of other kinds are running, a judge while an ingest runs, a pass whose `Kind` is `KindParks` while passes of every other kind run and each of them while one whose `Kind` is `KindParks` runs, and an enrich or an ingest whose `Origin` is `OriginIngest` while a pass whose `Kind` is `KindEnrichTarget` runs among them, and while passes of its own kind over other scopes are running: an ingest while an ingest of another run runs, an ingest whose `Origin` is `OriginIngest` and whose `Scraper` is `fmcsa` while one whose `Origin` is `OriginRun` runs and while one whose `Origin` is `OriginIngest` and whose `Scraper` is empty or `ppp` runs, and a pass whose `Kind` is `KindEnrichTarget` while one over another `Target` runs, a candidate's id and the id of a prospect list holding that candidate being two targets.
- R-P28J-XDYC: When `p` is a valid new pass and either `p.Delivery` is not empty and equal to the `Delivery` of a pass the store holds, or `p.Origin` is `OriginRun` and `p.Trigger` is equal to the `Trigger` of a pass the store holds whose `Origin` is `OriginRun`, whatever that pass's `Status`, `AddPass(ctx, p)` MUST return the zero `Pass` and an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrDelivered)` is true, and change no record; and otherwise, when the store holds a pass that blocks `p` (R-OHI9-FACJ), an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrBusy)` is true, changing no record; so that a running ingest whose `Origin` is `OriginIngest` and whose `Scraper` is empty refuses a second such ingest `ErrBusy`, a running one whose `Scraper` is `fmcsa` refuses another whose `Scraper` is `fmcsa` `ErrBusy`, a running judge refuses a new judge whose `Origin` is `OriginJudge` `ErrBusy` whatever the running one's origin, a running enrich refuses a second enrich `ErrBusy` whatever the two passes' origins, a running pass whose `Kind` is `KindParks` refuses a new one whose `Kind` is `KindParks` `ErrBusy` whatever the two passes' origins, a running pass whose `Kind` is `KindEnrichTarget` refuses another over the same `Target` `ErrBusy` whatever the two passes' `EnrichKind`, a done pass whose `Origin` is `OriginRun` refuses a new pass whose `Origin` is `OriginRun` with that pass's `Trigger` and another delivery `ErrDelivered`, and a done pass whose `Kind` is `KindJudge` and whose `Trigger` is `TriggerJudge` refuses no new pass whose `Kind` is `KindJudge` with that `Trigger` and another delivery, which a running one refuses `ErrBusy`.
- R-4SM4-Q4EK: When `p` is not a valid new pass, `AddPass(ctx, p)` MUST return the zero `Pass` and an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrUnreachable)` is true, read nothing from `Config.Rand`, and change no record.
- R-VROI-RVCG: When the store holds a pass whose `ID` is `id` and whose `Status` is `StatusRunning`, and no field of `c` is negative, `FinishPass(ctx, id, c)` MUST return a nil error and that pass with `Status` `StatusDone`, `Finished` equal to `Config.Now()` read during the call in UTC truncated to the second, each of its thirty-five counts equal to the field of `c` of the same name, and every other field unchanged; every later `GetPass` of it, on that store and on a store over a later handle for the same path, MUST return it; and every other record MUST be unchanged.
- R-VU4B-JETU: When the store holds a pass whose `ID` is `id` and whose `Status` is `StatusRunning`, and `reason` is `ReasonScrapes`, `ReasonInterrupted`, `ReasonStore`, `ReasonPrompts` or `ReasonPlaces`, `FailPass(ctx, id, reason)` MUST return a nil error and that pass with `Status` `StatusFailed`, `Reason` `reason`, `Finished` equal to `Config.Now()` read during the call in UTC truncated to the second, and every other field unchanged, the thirty-five counts 0 and `CeilingReached` false included; every later `GetPass` of it, on that store and on a store over a later handle for the same path, MUST return it; and every other record, every candidate, link, estimate, judgment, ruling, enrichment result, facts row, page, park, the park rules, every `Place` and every walk of a park (`D19-walk`) included, MUST be unchanged.
- R-OTP9-8ZRH: `FinishPass(ctx, id, c)` and `FailPass(ctx, id, reason)` MUST each return the zero `Pass` and: an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrUnreachable)` is true when a field of `c` is negative, for `FinishPass`, or when `reason` is not one of the five `Reason` constants, for `FailPass`; otherwise one for which `errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound)` is true when the store holds no pass whose `ID` is `id`; otherwise one for which `errors.Is(err, ErrEnded)` is true when that pass's `Status` is not `StatusRunning`; and in each case change no record.

- R-Q3Z2-0ZJF: candidates' design defines the passes **past keeping** at a `keep` of at least 1 as the passes the store holds whose `Status` is `StatusDone` and whose position, counting from 0, among the passes whose `Status` is `StatusDone`, in pass order, is `keep` or more, and the passes whose `Status` is `StatusFailed` and whose position among the passes whose `Status` is `StatusFailed`, in pass order, is `keep` or more, no pass whose `Status` is `StatusRunning` counting toward either position or being past keeping; every requirement of candidates' design that names the passes past keeping MUST denote these.
- R-Q6EU-SJ0T: When `keep` and `n` are each at least 1, `PrunePasses(ctx, keep, n)` MUST remove exactly the last `n`, or all of them when they number `n` or fewer, in pass order, of the passes past keeping at `keep` (R-Q3Z2-0ZJF) before the call, and return a nil error and the number it removed; afterwards `GetPass` of each removed pass, on that store and on a store over a later handle for the same path, MUST return an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound)` is true, an `AddPass` with the `Delivery` or the `Trigger` it carried MUST no longer be refused for it, and every other record, every candidate, link, estimate, judgment, ruling and drive time included, MUST be unchanged; so that with `keep` 2 and `n` 1 a store whose passes in pass order are one `running`, four `done` and two `failed` loses its fourth `done` pass at a first call and its third at a second, a third call returns 0 and removes nothing, and calls with one `keep` repeated until one returns 0 leave no pass past keeping at it.

- R-LAVT-59XG: `OpenProspectList(ctx, id, userID, userEmail)` MUST return an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrUnreachable)` is true when `userID` is empty, whatever `id` is, and otherwise one for which `errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound)` is true when the store holds no prospect list whose `ID` is `id`, an `id` that `ValidProspectListID` refuses and the `ID` of a deleted prospect list included; and whenever it returns a non-nil error, for these or any other reason, it MUST return the zero `ProspectListRead`, record no open and change no record.
- R-ZT7I-XW82: `Opens(ctx, prospectList)` MUST return a nil error and every open the store holds whose `ProspectList` is `prospectList`, each once, in descending order of `Seq`, whether or not the store holds a prospect list whose `ID` is `prospectList`, a deleted prospect list's opens included, and a non-nil slice of length zero when there is none; it MUST change no record and never return an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound)` is true; and every `Open` it returns MUST have an `At` whose location is `time.UTC` and whose nanoseconds are zero, `Filters` and `Order` equal to their own normal forms (R-ZPJT-SKZZ) and a non-nil `Members` and `Passing`; so that a prospect list never opened, an id the store never held and an id `ValidProspectListID` refuses each answer a slice of length zero.
- R-3BNI-31WH: When `n` is at least 1, `ListProspectLists(ctx, name, id, n)` MUST return a nil error and, each once and in prospect list order (R-3TXZ-TM0W), the first `n` of the **following** prospect lists, or all of them when they number `n` or fewer, each equal, field for field, to the prospect list `GetProspectList` of its `ID` answers at the call, and a non-nil slice of length zero when there is none; the following prospect lists being, when `id` is empty, every prospect list the store holds, whatever `name` is, and otherwise every prospect list the store holds whose `Name` is greater than `name` in byte order, or is equal to it and whose `ID` is greater than `id` in byte order, whether or not any prospect list's `Name` and `ID` are `name` and `id`; so that, over five prospect lists of which three share one `Name` and the two others have `Name`s greater in byte order, the lower of those two having an `ID` lower in byte order than every `ID` of the three, successive calls with `n` 2, the first with an empty `id` and each later one passing the `Name` and the `ID` of the last prospect list the call before returned, answer the five once each in prospect list order wherever the boundary between calls falls, and the call after the last prospect list answers none.
- R-S40R-GZGM: When `n` is at least 1, `ListProspectListsReversed(ctx, name, id, n)` MUST return a nil error and, in the reverse of prospect list order (R-3TXZ-TM0W), the first `n`, or all of them when they number `n` or fewer, of the **preceding** prospect lists, taken nearest the position first, each equal, field for field, to the prospect list `GetProspectList` of its `ID` answers at the call, and a non-nil slice of length zero when there is none; the preceding prospect lists being, when `id` is empty, every prospect list the store holds, whatever `name` is, and otherwise every prospect list the store holds whose `Name` is less than `name` in byte order, or is equal to it and whose `ID` is less than `id` in byte order, whether or not any prospect list's `Name` and `ID` are `name` and `id`; so that, over prospect lists `A`, `B` and `C` in prospect list order, `B` and `C` sharing one `Name`, `ListProspectListsReversed(ctx, "", "", 2)` returns `C` then `B`, a call passing `C`'s `Name` and `ID` with `n` 2 returns `B` then `A`, one passing `B`'s with `n` 5 returns `A` alone, one passing `A`'s returns none, and one passing `C`'s `Name` and an id between `B`'s and `C`'s in byte order with `n` 5 returns `B` then `A`.
- R-MCPU-FZEK: When `r.UserID` is not empty, `r.Kind` is `KindReject` or `KindUnreject`, `strings.TrimSpace(r.Why)` is not empty, and the store holds a prospect list whose `ID` is `r.ProspectList` and a candidate whose `ID` is `r.Candidate` and whose `Status` is `StatusActive`, `Reject(ctx, r)` MUST return a nil error and: when `r.Kind` is `KindReject` and the prospect list and candidate have no standing reject (`D14-prospect-lists`), or `r.Kind` is `KindUnreject` and they have one, record and return a reject record whose `Kind`, `ProspectList`, `Candidate`, `Why`, `UserID` and `UserEmail` equal `r`'s and whose `At` equals `Config.Now()` read during the call in UTC truncated to the second; when `r.Kind` is `KindReject` and they have a standing reject, record nothing and return the reject record that stands; and when `r.Kind` is `KindUnreject` and they have no standing reject, record nothing and return a `Reject` whose `Kind` is `KindUnreject`, whose `ProspectList` and `Candidate` equal `r`'s, whose `Why`, `UserID` and `UserEmail` are empty and whose `At` is the zero `time.Time`; the returned `ProspectListName` being that prospect list's `Name` in every case, whatever `r.ProspectListName` and `r.At` are; a record recorded MUST be the first `Rejects` of the candidate returns, on that store and on a store over a later handle for the same path, until a later `Reject` records another naming that candidate; no reject record MUST ever be changed or removed; and every other record MUST be unchanged; so that a reject repeated, whatever its why, records one record and answers it each time, and an unreject of a prospect list and candidate never rejected records nothing.
- R-P11W-NUZF: `Reject(ctx, r)` MUST return the zero `Reject` and: an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrUnreachable)` is true when `r.UserID` is empty or `r.Kind` is neither `KindReject` nor `KindUnreject`; otherwise one for which `errors.Is(err, ErrInvalid)` is true when `strings.TrimSpace(r.Why)` is empty; otherwise one for which `errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound)` is true when the store holds no prospect list whose `ID` is `r.ProspectList`, an `r.ProspectList` that `ValidProspectListID` refuses included; otherwise one for which `errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound)` is true when it holds no candidate whose `ID` is `r.Candidate`; otherwise one for which `errors.Is(err, ErrInvalid)` is true when that candidate's `Status` is `StatusMerged`; and in each case change no record.
- R-0UMU-5SRS: When the store holds a candidate whose `ID` is `candidate`, whatever its `Status`, `Rejects(ctx, candidate)` MUST return a nil error and every reject record the store holds whose `Candidate` is `candidate`, each once, the record a `Reject` call recorded later first, each with `ProspectListName` the `Name` of the prospect list whose `ID` is its `ProspectList` when the store holds that prospect list and empty otherwise, and a non-nil slice of length zero when there is none; and when it holds no such candidate, a nil slice and an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound)` is true.
- R-ZUFF-BNYR: Every `ProspectList` a method of `*Store` returns with a nil error, as a result, in a slice or in a `ProspectListRead`, MUST have a `Created` and a `Changed` whose location is `time.UTC` and whose nanoseconds are zero, a `Changed` not before its `Created`, and `Filters` and `Order` equal to their own normal forms (R-ZPJT-SKZZ); and every `Reject` one whose `Kind` is `KindReject` or `KindUnreject` and whose `At` is the zero `time.Time` or has the location `time.UTC` and zero nanoseconds.
- R-ZVNB-PFPG: Every `Candidate` a method of `*Store` returns with a nil error, as a result, in a slice, in a `Ranked` or in a `ProspectListRead`, MUST have a `Created` and an `Updated` whose location is `time.UTC` and whose nanoseconds are zero; so that a candidate's `Created` and an open's `At` (R-ZT7I-XW82) compare as whole UTC seconds.
- R-QZ2H-X82S: Every `Candidate` a method of `*Store` returns with a nil error, as a result, in a slice, in a `Ranked` or in a `ProspectListRead`, MUST have a non-nil `OtherNames`, `OtherAddresses`, `OtherPhones` and `OtherEmails`; so that a candidate whose records hold no other value, a merged candidate and a candidate the previous release wrote each answer them as slices of length zero, never nil.
- R-KTT7-SHJQ: Concurrent calls of `CreateProspectList`, `EditProspectList`, `DeleteProspectList`, `Reject` and `OpenProspectList` on one `*Store` MUST each return what it would return had the calls run one at a time in some order, and leave the records as that order leaves them; so that of several concurrent `Reject` calls of kind `KindReject` naming one prospect list and one active candidate with no standing reject, exactly one record is recorded and every call returns it, of two concurrent `DeleteProspectList` calls of one prospect list exactly one returns a nil error and the other an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound)` is true, and of two concurrent `OpenProspectList` calls of one prospect list of which no open is recorded, while `Config.Now` answers one time `t`, each returns a nil error, exactly one returns a `Changes` whose `Since` is the zero `time.Time` and the other one whose `Since` is `t` in UTC truncated to the second, and two opens are recorded.
- R-3IBT-RASF: A call of a method of `*Store` other than `OpenProspectList` MUST NOT record an open, and every call of a method of `*Store` MUST leave every open the store held before it as it was; so that `ReadProspectList`, `CreateProspectList`, `EditProspectList`, `DeleteProspectList`, `Reject`, `Decide`, `Judge`, `JudgeAll`, `AddEnrichments`, `PrunePasses` and every other call leave `Opens` of every prospect list id answering what it answered before the call, an `OpenProspectList` call adds at most one open, before the others in `Opens` of its prospect list, and no open is ever pruned or removed.
- R-6JJI-CJLD: candidates' design defines a **valid sort key** under a sort `s` as a string `k` for which `strconv.ParseInt(k, 10, 64)` returns a nil error, or, when the effective sort (R-TMFI-X33R) of `s` is `parks.SortMinutes`, the empty string; and, for a sort `s`, a valid sort key `k` under it and a non-empty id `i`, a `Park` `x` as **after** the position (`k`, `i`) under `s` when `x` follows, in the park order under `s` (R-TNNF-AUUG), a `Park` whose sort key under `s` is `k` and whose `ID` is `i`, and as **before** that position when `x` precedes such a `Park`; every requirement of candidates' design naming these MUST denote them; so that under `parks.SortMinutes` the parks after (`12`, `i`) are those of more than 12 minutes, those of 12 whose `ID` is greater than `i` and those of unknown minutes, and the parks after the empty key and `i` are those of unknown minutes whose `ID` is greater than `i`, while under `parks.SortValue` the parks after (`500`, `i`) are those whose `BuildingValue` is less than 500 and those of 500 whose `ID` is greater than `i`.
- R-64U1-LEO8: `ParkRules(ctx)` MUST return a nil error, the **stored rules** and, as a non-nil slice, the counties known (R-EP8R-Z5D9), and change no record; the stored rules being the `parks.Rules` whose `Distance`, `MinParcels` and `ValueThreshold` are each the value the newest `SetParkRules` that returned a nil error and set it gave, or the seed rules' (R-TRB4-G62J) while none has, and whose `Labels` maps each county the store holds at least one label row of, and no other county, to its labels in the order the newest such `SetParkRules` that replaced that county's label rows gave them, or in the seed rules' order while none has; so that on a database `0023_parks.sql` has just migrated it answers the seed rules and the nine counties of the seed rules in byte order, after an edit setting the distance to 800 and `Carlton`'s labels to one label it answers that distance, that label for `Carlton` and `Carlton` among the counties, and after an edit giving `Chisago` an empty `Labels` its `Labels` holds no `Chisago` key and its counties hold `Chisago` only while the newest applied derivation read it.
- R-W07T-G9JB: When `p` is a valid new pass (R-OHI9-FACJ) whose `Kind` is `KindParks` and whose `Origin` is `OriginRules`; each of `e.Distance`, `e.MinParcels` and `e.ValueThreshold` is nil or points to a value that `parks.ValidDistance`, `parks.ValidMinParcels` or `parks.ValidValueThreshold` respectively accepts; either `e.County` is nil and `e.Labels` holds no element, or `e.County` points to a value `parks.ValidCounty` accepts and `parks.ValidLabels(e.Labels)` is true; `e.WalkCeiling` is nil or points to a value `places.ValidCeiling` accepts (`D19-walk`); and the store holds no pass that blocks `p`, `SetParkRules(ctx, e, p)` MUST, in one write, make the stored rules (R-64U1-LEO8) the **edited rules**, the stored rules with `Distance`, `MinParcels` and `ValueThreshold` replaced by each of `*e.Distance`, `*e.MinParcels` and `*e.ValueThreshold` that is not nil and, when `e.County` is not nil, the label rows of `*e.County` replaced by one row for each element of `e.Labels`, in its order, and none when it holds no element; make what `WalkCeiling` answers `*e.WalkCeiling` when `e.WalkCeiling` is not nil (`D19-walk`); record `p` as `AddPass(ctx, p)` would (R-VP8Q-0BV2); and return a nil error and the `Pass` that `AddPass` would return; every later `ParkRules` and `WalkCeiling`, on that store and on a store over a later handle for the same path, MUST answer the edited rules and that walk ceiling until a later call changes them; and every other record, every park, `ParkCountiesRead`, `ParksRun` and, when `e.WalkCeiling` is nil, what `WalkCeiling` answers included, MUST be unchanged; so that an edit whose every field is nil records the pass and leaves the rules as they were, an edit giving `St. Louis` the one label `Industrial Non-Preferred` leaves that county that label alone, and an edit whose only non-nil field is `WalkCeiling`, pointing to 250, records the pass, leaves what `ParkRules` answers as it was and makes `WalkCeiling` answer 250.
- R-OR9G-HGA3: `SetParkRules(ctx, e, p)` MUST return the zero `Pass`, change no record, the rules and the walk ceiling included, and: an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrUnreachable)` is true when `p` is not a valid new pass (R-OHI9-FACJ) whose `Kind` is `KindParks` and whose `Origin` is `OriginRules`, reading nothing from `Config.Rand`; otherwise one for which `errors.Is(err, ErrInvalid)` is true when one of `e.Distance`, `e.MinParcels` and `e.ValueThreshold` points to a value that `parks.ValidDistance`, `parks.ValidMinParcels` or `parks.ValidValueThreshold` respectively refuses, `e.County` points to a value `parks.ValidCounty` refuses, `e.County` is nil and `e.Labels` holds at least one element, `parks.ValidLabels(e.Labels)` is false, or `e.WalkCeiling` points to a value `places.ValidCeiling` (`D19-walk`) refuses; and otherwise one for which `errors.Is(err, ErrBusy)` is true when the store holds a pass that blocks `p`; so that, while a pass whose `Kind` is `KindParks` runs, whatever its origin, an edit setting the distance to 800 is refused `ErrBusy` and `ParkRules` answers the distance it answered before, an edit setting it to 0 is refused `ErrInvalid` whether or not one runs, and so is one setting the walk ceiling to 0 or to 5,001, `WalkCeiling` answering afterwards what it answered before.
- R-5YT7-UFZK: When `n` is at least 1, `ActiveAddressKeys(ctx, after, n)` MUST return a nil error; a non-nil slice holding, in ascending byte order of `ID`, `parks.AddressKey(c.Street, c.Zip)` of each candidate `c` of its page for which that is not empty, and nothing else, its page being the first `n`, or all of them when they number `n` or fewer, in ascending byte order of `ID`, of the candidates the store holds when the call begins whose `Status` is `StatusActive` and whose `ID` is greater than `after` in byte order; and the `ID` of the last candidate of its page, or an empty string when its page is empty; and it MUST change no record; when `n` is less than 1 it MUST return a nil slice, an empty string and an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrUnreachable)` is true; so that successive calls, the first with an empty `after` and each later one passing the id the call before returned, ending with the first that returns an empty string, answer between them, over a store no call changes meanwhile, exactly the candidate keys (R-TOVB-OML5), and a page none of whose candidates has a ZIP answers an empty slice and a non-empty id.
- R-6014-87Q9: `PriorParks(ctx)` MUST return a nil error and a non-nil slice holding one `parks.Prior` for each park the store holds, whatever its `State`, in ascending byte order of `ID`, whose `ID`, `County`, `Town`, `State`, `Number`, `GrownFrom` and `Created` are that park's as `GetPark` answers them, whose `Count` is its `Parcels`, and whose `PINs` are the `PIN` of each parcel `ParkParcels` answers for it, in ascending byte order; and change no record; so that a gone park's prior carries the parcels it kept when it went.

for each element `w` of `plan.News`, in order, add a park whose `ID` it mints (R-56RJ-1PXL), whose `County`, `Town`, `Zip`, `Lat`, `Lon`, `Radius` and `BuildingValue` are those of `w.Cluster`, whose `Number` is `w.Number`, whose `Parcels` is `len(w.Cluster.Parcels)`, whose `GrownFrom` is 0, whose `State` is `parks.StateActive`, which is unwalked, whose `Run` is `run`, whose `Created` and `Updated` are `now`, and whose parcels are exactly the parcels of `w.Cluster`; for each element of `plan.Gone`, make the park of that `ID` `parks.StateGone` with `Updated` `now`, its parcels and every other field as they were; leave every other park as it was; make `ParkCountiesRead` answer the distinct elements of `counties` in byte order and `ParksRun` answer `run`; end the pass `pass` as `FinishPass(ctx, pass, c)` would (R-VROI-RVCG) and with `ParcelRun` `run`; and return a nil error and that pass; the parcels of a cluster being, for each element `q` of its `Parcels`, the `ParkParcel` whose `PIN`, `Street`, `City`, `Zip`, `BuildingValue` and `Rule` are `q`'s, whose `Owners` is `q.Owners`, a non-nil empty slice when that is nil, and whose `Lat` and `Lon` are `*q.Lat` and `*q.Lon`; every later read, on that store and on a store over a later handle for the same path, MUST answer accordingly until a later call changes it; and the stored rules (R-64U1-LEO8) and every record other than the parks, their parcels, what `ParkCountiesRead` and `ParksRun` answer and that pass MUST be unchanged; so that a plan with no element leaves every park as it was and still records `counties`, `run` and the counts, an updated park keeps its id, name and number while its parcels are replaced, and a gone park keeps its parcels.
- R-62GW-ZR7N: `ApplyParks(ctx, pass, run, counties, plan, c)` MUST return the zero `Pass`, change no record and: an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrUnreachable)` is true when `run` is empty, a field of `c` is negative, a parcel of a cluster of `plan` has a nil `Lat` or `Lon` or a `Rule` that is none of `parks.RuleLabel`, `parks.RuleValue` and `parks.RuleCandidate`, the `Parcels` of a cluster of `plan` hold two elements with one `PIN`, the `Number` of an element of `plan.News` is less than 1, an element of `plan.Gone` or the `ID` of an element of `plan.Updates` is not the `ID` of a park the store holds, or one id is among those twice; otherwise one for which `errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound)` is true when the store holds no pass whose `ID` is `pass` and whose `Kind` is `KindParks`; otherwise one for which `errors.Is(err, ErrEnded)` is true when that pass's `Status` is not `StatusRunning`; and otherwise one for which `errors.Is(err, ErrUnreachable)` is true when minting an id fails (R-56RJ-1PXL); so that an `ApplyParks` after a `FailPass` of its pass leaves every park, `ParkCountiesRead` and `ParksRun` as they were, one naming a running pass whose `Kind` is `KindIngest` changes nothing, and one whose plan holds a new park numbered 0, or a cluster listing one PIN twice, is refused and leaves its pass running.
- R-W7J7-QVZH: `ParkCountiesRead(ctx)` MUST return a nil error and the counties the newest `ApplyParks` that returned a nil error made it answer (R-W3VI-LKRE), a non-nil empty slice while none has; and `ParksRun(ctx)` a nil error and the run that call made it answer, an empty string while none has; each MUST change no record, and what each answers MUST stay as it is when a pass, the one that call ended included, is pruned; so that the run the parks were last derived from outlives the pass that derived it.
- R-64WP-RAP1: When `n` is at least 1 and `ListParks(ctx, f, sort, key, id, n)` meets none of the conditions R-67CI-IU6F refuses, it MUST return a nil error and, each once and in the park order under `sort` (R-TNNF-AUUG), the first `n` of the **following** parks, or all of them when they number `n` or fewer, each equal, field for field, to the park `GetPark` of its `ID` answers at the call, and a non-nil slice of length zero when there is none; the following parks being the parks the store holds that pass the park filter `f` (R-TMFI-X33R) and, when `id` is not empty, are after the position (`key`, `id`) under `sort` (R-6JJI-CJLD), whether or not a park's sort key and `ID` are `key` and `id`, and every park passing `f` when `id` is empty, whatever `key` is; and it MUST change no record; so that, under each sort, successive calls with `n` 2, the first with an empty `id` and each later one passing the sort key under `sort` and the `ID` of the last park the call before returned, answer every park passing `f` once, in the park order under `sort`, wherever the boundaries between calls fall, parks of unknown minutes last under `parks.SortMinutes`, and the call after the last park answers none.

- R-RYYW-L4WX: candidates' design defines the **place record** of a link `l` the store holds whose `Scraper` is `places.Name` (`D19-walk`) as the place the store holds whose `Walk` is `l.Walk` and whose `PlaceID` is `l.SourceID`, the record the newest walk that found its place made; and the **Places URL** of a candidate `c` the store holds as `strings.TrimSpace(w)`, `w` being the `Website` of the place record of the first link, in the order `Links` answers `c`'s links (R-2HQL-5ILH), whose `Scraper` is `places.Name` and whose place record has a `Website` `w` for which `strings.TrimSpace(w)` is an offered URL (R-LTXN-K82G, `D15-website-check`), a URL `website.Inputs` offers the check, and as the empty string when it has no such link; every requirement of candidates' design naming a link's place record or a candidate's Places URL MUST denote these; so that a candidate whose one places link's place record has the `Website` ` https://acme.test/ ` has the Places URL `https://acme.test/`; one whose places link was found again by a later walk that recorded its place with an empty `Website` has an empty Places URL, though an earlier walk recorded the place with one; one holding two places links has the URL of the one `Links` answers first among those whose place record's website is an offered URL; one whose only places link's place record has the `Website` `acme.test`, `ftp://acme.test/` or `https://10.0.0.1/` has an empty Places URL; and one holding no places link has an empty Places URL.
- R-BFUH-DS25: The `internal/store` package MUST export the method `PlacesURL(ctx context.Context, candidate string) (string, error)` on `*Store`.
- R-D0LQ-4QEX: When the store holds a candidate whose `ID` is `candidate`, whatever its `Status`, `PlacesURL(ctx, candidate)` MUST return a nil error and that candidate's Places URL (R-RYYW-L4WX) in the records the store holds at the call; when it holds none, the empty string and an error for which `errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound)` is true; and it MUST change no record; so that a candidate a walk's `RecordSweep` founded from a place whose website is `https://acme.test/` answers `https://acme.test/`, and one holding no places link the empty string.
