- R-D1TM-II5M: candidates' design defines the **facts projection** of a candidate `c` the store holds as the `Facts` whose `Candidate` is `c.ID`, and whose other fields are these, `m` and `l` being what `json.Unmarshal` decodes the `Found` of its newest result (R-GSBL-OU6L) of `enrichers.KindDriveMinutes` and of `enrichers.KindLinkFacts` into, an `enrichers.MinutesFound` and an `enrichers.LinkFound` respectively (`D16-enrichers`), its **website decision** being the website decision (R-QJ7S-Y7FR) of the slice `Enrichments` of `c` answers (R-GR3P-B2FW) at the Places URL of `c` (R-RYYW-L4WX), its **newest good brief** being the first `Enrichment` in the slice `Enrichments` of `c` answers (R-GR3P-B2FW) whose `Kind` is `enrichers.KindBrief` and whose `Found` `f` gives `p.Failed(f)` false, `p` being the `enrichers.Prompter` (R-7NKZ-ZJ2M) that `Get(enrichers.KindBrief)` of the registry `enrichers.New(enrichers.Config{})` returns (R-L7WQ-2OAH), and `b` being what `json.Unmarshal` decodes the `Found` of its newest good brief into, an `enrichers.BriefFound` (R-L0LB-S1UB): `Minutes` is `m.Minutes`; `KnownSince` and `NewestFiling` are `l.KnownSince` and `l.NewestFiling`, each as `time.Parse` with the layout `2006-01-02` reads it, and the zero `time.Time` when it is empty; `KnownSinceSource` and `NewestFilingSource` are `l.KnownSinceSource` and `l.NewestFilingSource`; `GovernmentMatch` is `l.GovernmentMatch`; `Records` is a non-nil slice holding, once each, in ascending byte order of `Name` and, among equal `Name`, in ascending byte order of `Source`, every distinct `sources.Category`, two being distinct when their `Name` or their `Source` differ, among these: when `c` has a newest result of `enrichers.KindLinkFacts`, for each element `x` of `l.Categories`, the one whose `Name` and `Source` are `x.Name` and `x.Source`; `Categories` is, when `c` has a newest good brief and `b.Categories` has at least one element, a non-nil slice holding, once each, in ascending byte order of `Name`, every distinct `sources.Category` whose `Name` is the `Name` of an element of `b.Categories` and whose `Source` is `enrichers.KindBrief`, and otherwise equal to `Records`; `FleetVerdict`, `PowerUnits` and `Drivers` are `l`'s fields of those names; `Website`, `WebsiteURL`, `WebsiteSource` and `WebsiteCheckedURL` are the state, the URL, the source and the checked URL of its website decision; `Brief` is `b.Summary` when `c` has a newest good brief and empty otherwise; every field a kind gives but `Categories`, `Records`, `Brief`, `Website`, `WebsiteURL`, `WebsiteSource` and `WebsiteCheckedURL` taking, when `c` has no newest result of that kind, its **unknown** value: the zero `time.Time`, an empty string, nil and `sources.FleetUnknown` for `FleetVerdict`; the unknown value of `Categories` and of `Records` being a non-nil slice of length zero, that of `Brief`, of `WebsiteURL`, of `WebsiteSource` and of `WebsiteCheckedURL` an empty string and that of `Website` `sources.WebsiteUnknown`, which the rules above give for `Records` when `c` has no newest result of `enrichers.KindLinkFacts`, for `Categories` and `Brief` when it has neither that nor a newest good brief, and for `Website`, `WebsiteURL`, `WebsiteSource` and `WebsiteCheckedURL` whenever its website decision gives them; and `Written` holding, in ascending byte order of the kind, one `Written` for each kind of which `c` has a newest result, whose `Kind` is that kind and whose `At` is that result's `At`, and nil when it has none; every requirement of candidates' design that names a candidate's facts projection, its newest good brief, its website decision or a fact's unknown value MUST denote these; so that a candidate with no result of `enrichers.KindBrief` has an empty `Brief`, no category whose `Source` is `enrichers.KindBrief` and no `Written` whose `Kind` is `enrichers.KindBrief`; one whose every result of `enrichers.KindBrief` is failed has an empty `Brief` and no such category, and has that `Written`, at its newest such result's `At`; a good brief whose categories hold `sources.CategoryRetail` twice gives that category once; a candidate whose newest `link_facts` result's categories are `sources.CategoryConstruction` from `ppp.Name` and `sources.CategoryTrucking` from `fmcsa.Name`, and which has no result of `enrichers.KindBrief`, has `Categories` and `Records` each exactly those two, in that order, which `ClassifiedBy` gives as `enrichers.SourceLinks`; once a good brief whose categories are `sources.CategoryRetail` and `sources.CategoryAgriculture` is recorded on it, its `Categories` are exactly `sources.CategoryAgriculture` from `enrichers.KindBrief` and `sources.CategoryRetail` from `enrichers.KindBrief`, in that order, which `ClassifiedBy` gives as `enrichers.KindBrief`, its `Brief` is that brief's summary and its `Records` are still the two link categories; once a failed brief is recorded after that good brief, its `Categories` are still exactly `sources.CategoryAgriculture` and `sources.CategoryRetail` from `enrichers.KindBrief`, its `Brief` still that good brief's summary, and only the `Written` of `enrichers.KindBrief` moves, to the failed result's `At`; once a later good brief whose categories are empty is recorded, its `Categories` are the two link categories again, which `ClassifiedBy` gives as `enrichers.SourceLinks`, and its `Brief` is the later brief's summary; once a failed brief is recorded after that, its `Categories`, `Records` and `Brief` are as they were and only the `Written` of `enrichers.KindBrief` moves, to the failed result's `At`; and a candidate whose newest `link_facts` result found no category and which has no good brief with one has `Categories` and `Records` each of length zero, which `ClassifiedBy` gives as the empty string; and a candidate whose newest `website_check` result's state is `sources.WebsiteNone` and which has a confirmed search has as `Website`, `WebsiteURL` and `WebsiteSource` that search's confirmation's state and URL and `WebsiteBySearch`, and a `Written` whose `Kind` is `enrichers.KindWebsiteSearch`, at its newest such result's `At`, whether or not that result is the confirmed search; and a candidate whose newest `website_check` result's state is `sources.WebsiteNone` and which has no result of `enrichers.KindWebsiteSearch` has `Website` `sources.WebsiteUnknown` and an empty `WebsiteURL` and `WebsiteSource`, and once a search whose answer's URL is empty is recorded on it, `Website` `sources.WebsiteNone`, still with an empty `WebsiteURL` and `WebsiteSource`, and a `Written` whose `Kind` is `enrichers.KindWebsiteSearch`, at that search's `At`; and a candidate whose newest `website_check` result's state is `sources.WebsiteHTTPS` and whose URL is `https://acme.test/`, and which holds a search whose `Case` is `enrichers.SearchCaseDifferent`, whose `Website` is `https://acme.test/` and whose confirmation's state is `sources.WebsiteHTTPS`, has as `Website`, `WebsiteURL` and `WebsiteSource` that search's confirmation's state and URL and `WebsiteBySearch`, whichever of the two was recorded later, and still has them once a search whose `Case` is `enrichers.SearchCaseStands` is recorded on it; and a candidate whose newest `link_facts` result's `KnownSinceSource` is `form5500.Name` and `NewestFilingSource` `fmcsa.Name` has those as its `KnownSinceSource` and `NewestFilingSource`, and both empty once a newer `link_facts` result recorded by an earlier release, whose `Found` has neither member, is recorded on it; and a candidate holding a places link whose place record (R-RYYW-L4WX) has the `Website` `https://acme.test/` and, as its only result of `sources.WebsiteCheck`, one whose `Inputs` are `acme-mail.test` and whose state is `sources.WebsiteNone` has `Website` `sources.WebsiteUnknown`, `WebsiteSource` `WebsiteByPlaces` and an empty `WebsiteURL` and `WebsiteCheckedURL`, and once a result of `sources.WebsiteCheck` whose `Inputs` are what `website.Inputs("https://acme.test/", "ops@acme-mail.test")` returns, whose state is `sources.WebsiteHTTPS`, whose URL is `https://acme.test/` and whose `From` is `https://acme.test/` is recorded on it, `Website` `sources.WebsiteHTTPS`, `WebsiteSource` `WebsiteByCheck` and `WebsiteURL` and `WebsiteCheckedURL` each `https://acme.test/`.
- R-EFTI-73K3: candidates' design defines the **drive decision** of a candidate `c` the store holds, at an origin `o`, as its **minutes**, **source**, **miles** and **route address**, these, its **deciding route** being the first `Enrichment` in the slice `Enrichments` of `c` answers (R-GR3P-B2FW) whose `Kind` is `enrichers.KindDriveRoute`, whose `Inputs` equal `c`'s inputs for `enrichers.KindDriveRoute` (R-OO40-CCZA) for the subject of `c` whose `Origin` is `o`, and whose `Found` `json.Unmarshal` decodes into an `enrichers.RouteFound` `g` (`D16-enrichers`) whose `Failed()` is false and whose `Minutes` is not nil, and `c` having none when no element is such: when `c` has a deciding route, the minutes are a pointer to the value `g.Minutes` points to, the source `MinutesByRoute`, the miles `g.Miles` and the route address `g.Address`; otherwise the minutes are the `Minutes` R-D1TM-II5M states, the source `MinutesByZip` when those minutes are not nil and empty otherwise, the miles nil and the route address empty. In addition to what R-D1TM-II5M states, and in place of what it states for `Minutes`, the facts projection of `c` MUST have as `Minutes`, `MinutesSource`, `Miles` and `RouteAddress` the minutes, source, miles and route address of `c`'s drive decision at the `Origin` of the `Config` (R-47BB-A47P) of the store on which the call of `AddEnrichments`, `Derive`, `DeriveAmong`, `FillFacts` or `Decide` that the requirement naming the projection states was made, or, when that requirement names no such call, of the store whose answer it states; the unknown value of `MinutesSource` and of `RouteAddress` being an empty string and that of `Miles` nil, which the drive decision gives when `c` has neither a deciding route nor a newest result of `enrichers.KindDriveMinutes` whose minutes are not nil; every requirement of candidates' design that names a candidate's drive decision or its deciding route MUST denote these; so that a candidate whose every result of `enrichers.KindDriveRoute` is failed, found no minutes or was found from inputs other than its inputs now takes its minutes from its newest `drive_minutes` result, with `MinutesSource` `MinutesByZip` when that result found minutes and empty when it found none, a deciding route older than a failed or unroutable `drive_route` result with the same inputs still decides, and every result of `enrichers.KindDriveRoute` gives its `Written` element as R-D1TM-II5M states, whether or not it decides.

- R-DS8O-A9S0: candidates' design defines a **write unit** as the write of a call of `Attach`, `MarkGone`, `Derive`, `JudgeAll`, `FillFacts`, `Rebuild`, `PrunePasses` or `ApplyParks`, each call writing in one transaction, and nothing else; and defines a write of a write unit as **busy** when the database refuses it because the write lock it needs is held, the driver answering an error whose result code's low eight bits are 5 (`SQLITE_BUSY`), as it answers a write once another connection has held the database's write lock for the busy timeout appkit's `db.Open` sets; every requirement of candidates' design that names a write unit or a busy write MUST denote these.

- R-02MI-PTTR: When, on a database every file of the root package's `Migrations()` has migrated, on which `Attach` has recorded candidates `c` and `d` and `AddPage` has recorded on `c` a page `q` whose `Reason` is not empty, `DB.Write` calls insert rows into `pages` naming exactly the columns the files `0001_candidates.sql` to `0026_fact_sources.sql` give it but `seq`, one with `size` 262144 among them, and update `pages` setting only `candidate`, to `d`'s `ID` where it is `c`'s, each as the previous release's store writes them, and a `DB.Read` call selects, by `seq`, exactly the columns those files give `pages` of `q`'s row, each such call MUST return a nil error, and the `text` that `DB.Read` call selects MUST be empty; and a store over it MUST then answer `GetPage` of each inserted page with its `Reason` empty, `GetPage(ctx, d.ID, q.Number)` with `q`'s `Reason`, and `PendingRefetches(ctx, 0, n)`, for every `n` at least 1, with no inserted page; so that the previous release, run on `0027_page_reasons.sql` after a rollback, records pages this release reads as readable, adds nothing to the work list, keeps a page's reason when it moves the page, and reads an unreadable page as one with an empty text.
